#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular

bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // A is stored in row-major order
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            
            if (i == j) {
                // Diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
                if (val <= 0.0) {
                    // Matrix is not positive definite
                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
                    return false;
                }
                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
            } else {
                // Off-diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
            }
        }
        
        // Zero out upper triangular part
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix
void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random
    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite
    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite
    
    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    
    // Generate random matrix B
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
    }
    
    // Compute A = B * B^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];
            }
            A[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        A[i * n + i] += n;
    }
}

bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix
    
    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);
    
    // Compute L * L^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];
            }
            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Compare with original
    double maxError = 0.0;
    double relError = 0.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);
        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);
        
        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);
        relError = std::max(relError, rel);
    }
    
    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", maxError);
    printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", relError);
    
    // Check if error is within tolerance
    if (relError > 1e-6) {
        printf("Validation failed: relative error too large\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t n = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            n = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Cholesky Decomposition Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", n, n);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrix
    std::vector<double> A(n * n);
    std::vector<double> A_orig;
    
    // Generate positive definite matrix
    printf("Generating positive definite matrix...\n");
    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(A, n);
    
    if (validate) {
        A_orig = A; // Save original for validation
    }
    
    // Perform Cholesky decomposition
    printf("Computing Cholesky decomposition...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    bool success = choleskyDecomposition(A, n);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    if (!success) {
        printf("Cholesky decomposition failed\n");
        return 1;
    }
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS (approximately n³/3 operations for Cholesky)
    double ops = (double)n * n * n / 3.0;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(A, "CholeskyL");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateCholesky(A, A_orig, n);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

#include <cstdio>

#include <vector>

// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular

bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // A is stored in row-major order
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            
            if (i == j) {
                // Diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
                if (val <= 0.0) {
                    // Matrix is not positive definite
                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
                    return false;
                }
                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
            } else {
                // Off-diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
            }
        }
        
        // Zero out upper triangular part
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix
void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random
    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite
    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite
    
    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    
    // Generate random matrix B
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
    }
    
    // Compute A = B * B^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];
            }
            A[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        A[i * n + i] += n;
    }
}

bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix
    
    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);
    
    // Compute L * L^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];
            }
            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Compare with original
    double maxError = 0.0;
    double relError = 0.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);
        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);
        
        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);
        relError = std::max(relError, rel);
    }
    
    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", maxError);
    printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", relError);
    
    // Check if error is within tolerance
    if (relError > 1e-6) {
        printf("Validation failed: relative error too large\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

    if (printResults) {
        print_results(A, "CholeskyL");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateCholesky(A, A_orig, n);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

codex
The factorization has dependencies between rows, so a simple parallel loop would be incorrect. I’ll use blocked Cholesky with OpenMP task dependencies to let independent matrix tiles run concurrently, and parallelize matrix generation and validation while preserving the seeded input sequence.

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular

bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // A is stored in row-major order
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            
            if (i == j) {
                // Diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
                if (val <= 0.0) {
                    // Matrix is not positive definite
                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
                    return false;
                }
                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
            } else {
                // Off-diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
            }
        }
        
        // Zero out upper triangular part
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix
void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random
    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite
    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite
    
    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    
    // Generate random matrix B
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
    }
    
    // Compute A = B * B^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];
            }
            A[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        A[i * n + i] += n;
    }
}

bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix
    
    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);
    
    // Compute L * L^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];
            }
            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Compare with original
    double maxError = 0.0;
    double relError = 0.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);
        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);
        
        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);
        relError = std::max(relError, rel);
    }
    
    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", maxError);
    printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", relError);
    
    // Check if error is within tolerance
    if (relError > 1e-6) {
        printf("Validation failed: relative error too large\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t n = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            n = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Cholesky Decomposition Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", n, n);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrix
    std::vector<double> A(n * n);
    std::vector<double> A_orig;
    
    // Generate positive definite matrix
    printf("Generating positive definite matrix...\n");
    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(A, n);
    
    if (validate) {
        A_orig = A; // Save original for validation
    }
    
    // Perform Cholesky decomposition
    printf("Computing Cholesky decomposition...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    bool success = choleskyDecomposition(A, n);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    if (!success) {
        printf("Cholesky decomposition failed\n");
        return 1;
    }
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS (approximately n³/3 operations for Cholesky)
    double ops = (double)n * n * n / 3.0;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(A, "CholeskyL");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateCholesky(A, A_orig, n);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

+void solvePanel(double* matrix, size_t n, size_t rowFirst, size_t rowLast,
+                size_t first, size_t last) {
+    for (size_t i = rowFirst; i < rowLast; ++i) {
+        double* row = matrix + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = first; j < last; ++j) {
+            const double* diagonalRow = matrix + j * n;
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = first; k < j; ++k) {
+                sum += row[k] * diagonalRow[k];
+            }
+            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / diagonalRow[j];
+            matrix[j * n + i] = row[j];
+        }
+    }

+// A 4 x 8 register block reuses each panel load across several output entries.
+// Only tiles on/below the diagonal are updated; unused entries above the
+// diagonal inside a diagonal tile are harmless and simplify this hot kernel.
+void updateTile(double* matrix, size_t n, size_t rowFirst, size_t rowLast,
+                size_t colFirst, size_t colLast, size_t first, size_t last) {
+    size_t i = rowFirst;
+    for (; i + 4 <= rowLast; i += 4) {
+        double* row0 = matrix + i * n;
+        double* row1 = row0 + n;
+        double* row2 = row1 + n;
+        double* row3 = row2 + n;
+        size_t j = colFirst;
+        for (; j + 8 <= colLast; j += 8) {
+            double sum0[8] = {}, sum1[8] = {}, sum2[8] = {}, sum3[8] = {};
+            for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
+                const double* transposed = matrix + k * n + j;
+                const double a0 = row0[k], a1 = row1[k];
+                const double a2 = row2[k], a3 = row3[k];

+                    const double b = transposed[c];
+                    sum0[c] += a0 * b;
+                    sum1[c] += a1 * b;
+                    sum2[c] += a2 * b;
+                    sum3[c] += a3 * b;

             }
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
+                row0[j + c] -= sum0[c];
+                row1[j + c] -= sum1[c];
+                row2[j + c] -= sum2[c];
+                row3[j + c] -= sum3[c];
+            }
+        }
+        for (; j < colLast; ++j) {
+            const double* other = matrix + j * n;
+            double sum0 = 0.0, sum1 = 0.0, sum2 = 0.0, sum3 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum0,sum1,sum2,sum3)
+            for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
+                sum0 += row0[k] * other[k];
+                sum1 += row1[k] * other[k];
+                sum2 += row2[k] * other[k];
+                sum3 += row3[k] * other[k];
+            }
+            row0[j] -= sum0;
+            row1[j] -= sum1;
+            row2[j] -= sum2;
+            row3[j] -= sum3;
+        }

+            const double* transposed = matrix + k * n;
+            #pragma omp simd

+                row[j] -= a * transposed[j];
+            }
         }

+}

+// Tiled, right-looking Cholesky. Dependencies are keyed by each lower tile's
+// first element; all tasks are siblings generated by the same single region.
+// There is no global barrier between panels, so the next diagonal can start
+// while independent trailing updates are still running.
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    double* matrix = A.data();
+    std::atomic<bool> failed{false};
+    size_t failedDiagonal = n;
+
+    #pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        #pragma omp single
+        {
+            for (size_t first = 0; first < n; first += tileSize) {
+                const size_t last = std::min(first + tileSize, n);
+                #pragma omp task firstprivate(first,last) \
+                    depend(inout:matrix[first*n+first]) shared(failed,failedDiagonal)
+                {
+                    if (!failed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                        const size_t bad = factorDiagonal(matrix, n, first, last);
+                        if (bad != n) {
+                            failedDiagonal = bad;
+                            failed.store(true, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+                for (size_t row = last; row < n; row += tileSize) {
+                    const size_t rowLast = std::min(row + tileSize, n);
+                    #pragma omp task firstprivate(row,rowLast,first,last) \
+                        depend(in:matrix[first*n+first]) \
+                        depend(inout:matrix[row*n+first]) shared(failed)
+                    {
+                        if (!failed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                            solvePanel(matrix, n, row, rowLast, first, last);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+                // Issue nearer diagonal tiles first to shorten the critical path.
+                for (size_t col = last; col < n; col += tileSize) {
+                    const size_t colLast = std::min(col + tileSize, n);
+                    for (size_t row = col; row < n; row += tileSize) {
+                        const size_t rowLast = std::min(row + tileSize, n);
+                        #pragma omp task firstprivate(row,rowLast,col,colLast,first,last) \
+                            depend(in:matrix[row*n+first],matrix[col*n+first]) \
+                            depend(inout:matrix[row*n+col]) shared(failed)
+                        {
+                            if (!failed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                                updateTile(matrix, n, row, rowLast, col, colLast, first, last);
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        // The single region's implicit barrier also waits for its tasks.
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            std::fill(matrix + i * n + i + 1, matrix + (i + 1) * n, 0.0);
+        }
+    }
+    if (failed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", failedDiagonal);
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    return true;
+}
+
+// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix.
+void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
+    unsigned int seed = 42;
+
+    // Keep the original random stream: changing seeds per thread changes A.
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
+        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
+    }
+
+    // Compute each symmetric pair once. Dynamic rows balance the triangular
+    // workload; SIMD reductions accelerate the contiguous inner products.
+    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic,1)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+        const double* row = B.data() + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
+            const double* other = B.data() + j * n;
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
+                sum += row[k] * other[k];
+            }
+            if (i == j) {
+                sum += n;
+            }
+            A[i * n + j] = sum;
+            A[j * n + i] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
+    double maxError = 0.0;
+    double relError = 0.0;
+
+    // L is lower triangular. Entries beyond min(i,j) contribute only zero;
+    // compare both symmetric entries directly without a reconstruction buffer.
+    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic,1) reduction(max:maxError,relError)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+        const double* row = L.data() + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
+            const double* other = L.data() + j * n;
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k <= j; ++k) {
+                sum += row[k] * other[k];
+            }
+            const double original = A_orig[i * n + j];
+            const double transposed = A_orig[j * n + i];
+            const double error = std::fabs(sum - original);
+            const double transposeError = std::fabs(sum - transposed);
+            maxError = std::max(maxError, std::max(error, transposeError));
+            relError = std::max(relError, error / (std::fabs(original) + 1e-10));
+            relError = std::max(relError, transposeError / (std::fabs(transposed) + 1e-10));
+        }
+    }
+
+    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", maxError);
+    printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", relError);
+
+    if (relError > 1e-6) {
+        printf("Validation failed: relative error too large\n");
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    return true;
+}

+void solvePanel(double* matrix, size_t n, size_t rowFirst, size_t rowLast,
+                size_t first, size_t last) {
+    for (size_t i = rowFirst; i < rowLast; ++i) {
+        double* row = matrix + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = first; j < last; ++j) {
+            const double* diagonalRow = matrix + j * n;
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = first; k < j; ++k) {
+                sum += row[k] * diagonalRow[k];
+            }
+            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / diagonalRow[j];
+            matrix[j * n + i] = row[j];
+        }
+    }

+// A 4 x 8 register block reuses each panel load across several output entries.
+// Only tiles on/below the diagonal are updated; unused entries above the
+// diagonal inside a diagonal tile are harmless and simplify this hot kernel.
+void updateTile(double* matrix, size_t n, size_t rowFirst, size_t rowLast,
+                size_t colFirst, size_t colLast, size_t first, size_t last) {
+    size_t i = rowFirst;
+    for (; i + 4 <= rowLast; i += 4) {
+        double* row0 = matrix + i * n;
+        double* row1 = row0 + n;
+        double* row2 = row1 + n;
+        double* row3 = row2 + n;
+        size_t j = colFirst;
+        for (; j + 8 <= colLast; j += 8) {
+            double sum0[8] = {}, sum1[8] = {}, sum2[8] = {}, sum3[8] = {};
+            for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
+                const double* transposed = matrix + k * n + j;
+                const double a0 = row0[k], a1 = row1[k];
+                const double a2 = row2[k], a3 = row3[k];
+                #pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
+                    const double b = transposed[c];
+                    sum0[c] += a0 * b;
+                    sum1[c] += a1 * b;
+                    sum2[c] += a2 * b;
+                    sum3[c] += a3 * b;
                 }
-                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
             }
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
+                row0[j + c] -= sum0[c];
+                row1[j + c] -= sum1[c];
+                row2[j + c] -= sum2[c];
+                row3[j + c] -= sum3[c];
+            }
+        }
+        for (; j < colLast; ++j) {
+            const double* other = matrix + j * n;
+            double sum0 = 0.0, sum1 = 0.0, sum2 = 0.0, sum3 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum0,sum1,sum2,sum3)
+            for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
+                sum0 += row0[k] * other[k];
+                sum1 += row1[k] * other[k];
+                sum2 += row2[k] * other[k];
+                sum3 += row3[k] * other[k];
+            }
+            row0[j] -= sum0;
+            row1[j] -= sum1;
+            row2[j] -= sum2;
+            row3[j] -= sum3;
+        }
+    }
+    for (; i < rowLast; ++i) {
+        double* row = matrix + i * n;
+        for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
+            const double a = row[k];
+            const double* transposed = matrix + k * n;
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t j = colFirst; j < colLast; ++j) {
+                row[j] -= a * transposed[j];
+            }
         }
-        
+    }
+}
+
+} // namespace
+
+// Tiled, right-looking Cholesky. Dependencies are keyed by each lower tile's
+// first element; all tasks are siblings generated by the same single region.
+// There is no global barrier between panels, so the next diagonal can start
+// while independent trailing updates are still running.
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    double* matrix = A.data();
+    std::atomic<bool> failed{false};
+    size_t failedDiagonal = n;
+
+    #pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        #pragma omp single
+        {
+            for (size_t first = 0; first < n; first += tileSize) {
+                const size_t last = std::min(first + tileSize, n);
+                #pragma omp task firstprivate(first,last) \
+                    depend(inout:matrix[first*n+first]) shared(failed,failedDiagonal)
+                {
+                    if (!failed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                        const size_t bad = factorDiagonal(matrix, n, first, last);
+                        if (bad != n) {
+                            failedDiagonal = bad;
+                            failed.store(true, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+                for (size_t row = last; row < n; row += tileSize) {
+                    const size_t rowLast = std::min(row + tileSize, n);
+                    #pragma omp task firstprivate(row,rowLast,first,last) \
+                        depend(in:matrix[first*n+first]) \
+                        depend(inout:matrix[row*n+first]) shared(failed)
+                    {
+                        if (!failed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                            solvePanel(matrix, n, row, rowLast, first, last);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+                // Issue nearer diagonal tiles first to shorten the critical path.
+                for (size_t col = last; col < n; col += tileSize) {
+                    const size_t colLast = std::min(col + tileSize, n);
+                    for (size_t row = col; row < n; row += tileSize) {
+                        const size_t rowLast = std::min(row + tileSize, n);
+                        #pragma omp task firstprivate(row,rowLast,col,colLast,first,last) \
+                            depend(in:matrix[row*n+first],matrix[col*n+first]) \
+                            depend(inout:matrix[row*n+col]) shared(failed)
+                        {
+                            if (!failed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                                updateTile(matrix, n, row, rowLast, col, colLast, first, last);
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        // The single region's implicit barrier also waits for its tasks.
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            std::fill(matrix + i * n + i + 1, matrix + (i + 1) * n, 0.0);
+        }
+    }
+    if (failed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", failedDiagonal);
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    return true;
+}
+
+// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix.
+void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
+    unsigned int seed = 42;
+
+    // Keep the original random stream: changing seeds per thread changes A.
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
+        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
+    }
+
+    // Compute each symmetric pair once. Dynamic rows balance the triangular
+    // workload; SIMD reductions accelerate the contiguous inner products.
+    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic,1)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+        const double* row = B.data() + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
+            const double* other = B.data() + j * n;
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
+                sum += row[k] * other[k];
+            }
+            if (i == j) {
+                sum += n;
+            }
+            A[i * n + j] = sum;
+            A[j * n + i] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
+    double maxError = 0.0;
+    double relError = 0.0;
+
+    // L is lower triangular. Entries beyond min(i,j) contribute only zero;
+    // compare both symmetric entries directly without a reconstruction buffer.
+    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic,1) reduction(max:maxError,relError)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+        const double* row = L.data() + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
+            const double* other = L.data() + j * n;
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k <= j; ++k) {
+                sum += row[k] * other[k];
+            }
+            const double original = A_orig[i * n + j];
+            const double transposed = A_orig[j * n + i];
+            const double error = std::fabs(sum - original);
+            const double transposeError = std::fabs(sum - transposed);
+            maxError = std::max(maxError, std::max(error, transposeError));
+            relError = std::max(relError, error / (std::fabs(original) + 1e-10));
+            relError = std::max(relError, transposeError / (std::fabs(transposed) + 1e-10));
+        }
+    }
+
+    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", maxError);
+    printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", relError);
+
+    if (relError > 1e-6) {
+        printf("Validation failed: relative error too large\n");
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    return true;
+}

+// while independent trailing updates are still running.
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    double* matrix = A.data();
+    std::atomic<bool> failed{false};
+    size_t failedDiagonal = n;
+
+    #pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        #pragma omp single
+        {
+            for (size_t first = 0; first < n; first += tileSize) {
+                const size_t last = std::min(first + tileSize, n);
+                #pragma omp task firstprivate(first,last) \
+                    depend(inout:matrix[first*n+first]) shared(failed,failedDiagonal)
+                {
+                    if (!failed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                        const size_t bad = factorDiagonal(matrix, n, first, last);
+                        if (bad != n) {
+                            failedDiagonal = bad;
+                            failed.store(true, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+                for (size_t row = last; row < n; row += tileSize) {
+                    const size_t rowLast = std::min(row + tileSize, n);
+                    #pragma omp task firstprivate(row,rowLast,first,last) \
+                        depend(in:matrix[first*n+first]) \
+                        depend(inout:matrix[row*n+first]) shared(failed)
+                    {
+                        if (!failed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                            solvePanel(matrix, n, row, rowLast, first, last);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+                // Issue nearer diagonal tiles first to shorten the critical path.
+                for (size_t col = last; col < n; col += tileSize) {
+                    const size_t colLast = std::min(col + tileSize, n);
+                    for (size_t row = col; row < n; row += tileSize) {
+                        const size_t rowLast = std::min(row + tileSize, n);
+                        #pragma omp task firstprivate(row,rowLast,col,colLast,first,last) \
+                            depend(in:matrix[row*n+first],matrix[col*n+first]) \
+                            depend(inout:matrix[row*n+col]) shared(failed)
+                        {
+                            if (!failed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                                updateTile(matrix, n, row, rowLast, col, colLast, first, last);
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        // The single region's implicit barrier also waits for its tasks.
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            std::fill(matrix + i * n + i + 1, matrix + (i + 1) * n, 0.0);
+        }
+    }
+    if (failed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", failedDiagonal);
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    return true;
+}
+
+// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix.
+void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
+    unsigned int seed = 42;
+
+    // Keep the original random stream: changing seeds per thread changes A.
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
+        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
+    }
+
+    // Compute each symmetric pair once. Dynamic rows balance the triangular
+    // workload; SIMD reductions accelerate the contiguous inner products.
+    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic,1)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+        const double* row = B.data() + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
+            const double* other = B.data() + j * n;
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
+                sum += row[k] * other[k];
+            }
+            if (i == j) {
+                sum += n;
+            }
+            A[i * n + j] = sum;
+            A[j * n + i] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
+    double maxError = 0.0;
+    double relError = 0.0;
+
+    // L is lower triangular. Entries beyond min(i,j) contribute only zero;
+    // compare both symmetric entries directly without a reconstruction buffer.
+    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic,1) reduction(max:maxError,relError)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+        const double* row = L.data() + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
+            const double* other = L.data() + j * n;
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k <= j; ++k) {
+                sum += row[k] * other[k];
+            }
+            const double original = A_orig[i * n + j];
+            const double transposed = A_orig[j * n + i];
+            const double error = std::fabs(sum - original);
+            const double transposeError = std::fabs(sum - transposed);
+            maxError = std::max(maxError, std::max(error, transposeError));
+            relError = std::max(relError, error / (std::fabs(original) + 1e-10));
+            relError = std::max(relError, transposeError / (std::fabs(transposed) + 1e-10));
+        }
+    }
+
+    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", maxError);
+    printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", relError);
+
+    if (relError > 1e-6) {
+        printf("Validation failed: relative error too large\n");
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    return true;
+}

+void solvePanel(double* matrix, size_t n, size_t rowFirst, size_t rowLast,
+                size_t first, size_t last) {
+    for (size_t i = rowFirst; i < rowLast; ++i) {
+        double* row = matrix + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = first; j < last; ++j) {
+            const double* diagonalRow = matrix + j * n;
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = first; k < j; ++k) {
+                sum += row[k] * diagonalRow[k];
+            }
+            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / diagonalRow[j];
+            matrix[j * n + i] = row[j];
+        }
+    }
+}

+// A 4 x 8 register block reuses each panel load across several output entries.
+// Only tiles on/below the diagonal are updated; unused entries above the
+// diagonal inside a diagonal tile are harmless and simplify this hot kernel.
+void updateTile(double* matrix, size_t n, size_t rowFirst, size_t rowLast,
+                size_t colFirst, size_t colLast, size_t first, size_t last) {
+    size_t i = rowFirst;
+    for (; i + 4 <= rowLast; i += 4) {
+        double* row0 = matrix + i * n;
+        double* row1 = row0 + n;
+        double* row2 = row1 + n;
+        double* row3 = row2 + n;
+        size_t j = colFirst;
+        for (; j + 8 <= colLast; j += 8) {

+            for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {

+                    const double b = transposed[c];
+                    sum0[c] += a0 * b;
+                    sum1[c] += a1 * b;
+                    sum2[c] += a2 * b;
+                    sum3[c] += a3 * b;

+                row1[j + c] -= sum1[c];
+                row2[j + c] -= sum2[c];
+                row3[j + c] -= sum3[c];
+            }
+        }

+            double sum0 = 0.0, sum1 = 0.0, sum2 = 0.0, sum3 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum0,sum1,sum2,sum3)
+            for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
+                sum0 += row0[k] * other[k];
+                sum1 += row1[k] * other[k];
+                sum2 += row2[k] * other[k];
+                sum3 += row3[k] * other[k];
+            }
+            row0[j] -= sum0;
+            row1[j] -= sum1;
+            row2[j] -= sum2;

+                row[j] -= a * transposed[j];
+            }

+
+// Tiled, right-looking Cholesky. Dependencies are keyed by each lower tile's
+// first element; all tasks are siblings generated by the same single region.
+// There is no global barrier between panels, so the next diagonal can start
+// while independent trailing updates are still running.
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    double* matrix = A.data();
+    std::atomic<bool> failed{false};
+    size_t failedDiagonal = n;
+
+    #pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        #pragma omp single
+        {
+            for (size_t first = 0; first < n; first += tileSize) {
+                const size_t last = std::min(first + tileSize, n);
+                #pragma omp task firstprivate(first,last) \
+                    depend(inout:matrix[first*n+first]) shared(failed,failedDiagonal)
+                {
+                    if (!failed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                        const size_t bad = factorDiagonal(matrix, n, first, last);
+                        if (bad != n) {
+                            failedDiagonal = bad;
+                            failed.store(true, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+                for (size_t row = last; row < n; row += tileSize) {
+                    const size_t rowLast = std::min(row + tileSize, n);
+                    #pragma omp task firstprivate(row,rowLast,first,last) \
+                        depend(in:matrix[first*n+first]) \
+                        depend(inout:matrix[row*n+first]) shared(failed)
+                    {
+                        if (!failed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                            solvePanel(matrix, n, row, rowLast, first, last);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+                // Issue nearer diagonal tiles first to shorten the critical path.
+                for (size_t col = last; col < n; col += tileSize) {
+                    const size_t colLast = std::min(col + tileSize, n);
+                    for (size_t row = col; row < n; row += tileSize) {
+                        const size_t rowLast = std::min(row + tileSize, n);
+                        #pragma omp task firstprivate(row,rowLast,col,colLast,first,last) \
+                            depend(in:matrix[row*n+first],matrix[col*n+first]) \
+                            depend(inout:matrix[row*n+col]) shared(failed)
+                        {
+                            if (!failed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                                updateTile(matrix, n, row, rowLast, col, colLast, first, last);
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        // The single region's implicit barrier also waits for its tasks.
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            std::fill(matrix + i * n + i + 1, matrix + (i + 1) * n, 0.0);
+        }
+    }
+    if (failed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", failedDiagonal);
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    return true;
+}
+
+// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix.
+void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
+    unsigned int seed = 42;
+
+    // Keep the original random stream: changing seeds per thread changes A.
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
+        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
+    }
+
+    // Compute each symmetric pair once. Dynamic rows balance the triangular
+    // workload; SIMD reductions accelerate the contiguous inner products.
+    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic,1)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+        const double* row = B.data() + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
+            const double* other = B.data() + j * n;
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
+                sum += row[k] * other[k];
+            }
+            if (i == j) {
+                sum += n;
+            }
+            A[i * n + j] = sum;
+            A[j * n + i] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
+    double maxError = 0.0;
+    double relError = 0.0;
+
+    // L is lower triangular. Entries beyond min(i,j) contribute only zero;
+    // compare both symmetric entries directly without a reconstruction buffer.
+    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic,1) reduction(max:maxError,relError)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+        const double* row = L.data() + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
+            const double* other = L.data() + j * n;
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k <= j; ++k) {
+                sum += row[k] * other[k];
+            }
+            const double original = A_orig[i * n + j];
+            const double transposed = A_orig[j * n + i];
+            const double error = std::fabs(sum - original);
+            const double transposeError = std::fabs(sum - transposed);
+            maxError = std::max(maxError, std::max(error, transposeError));
+            relError = std::max(relError, error / (std::fabs(original) + 1e-10));
+            relError = std::max(relError, transposeError / (std::fabs(transposed) + 1e-10));
+        }
+    }
+
+    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", maxError);
+    printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", relError);
+
+    if (relError > 1e-6) {
+        printf("Validation failed: relative error too large\n");
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    return true;
+}

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+namespace {
+
+constexpr size_t tileSize = 64;
 
-bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
-    // A is stored in row-major order
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {

+// The upper triangle temporarily holds the transpose of completed panels.
+// This lets the update kernel vectorize over contiguous columns without packing
+// another copy of the matrix. Every tile task also owns its transposed tile.
+size_t factorDiagonal(double* matrix, size_t n, size_t first, size_t last) {
+    for (size_t i = first; i < last; ++i) {
+        double* row = matrix + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = first; j <= i; ++j) {
+            const double* other = matrix + j * n;
             double sum = 0.0;
-            
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = first; k < j; ++k) {
+                sum += row[k] * other[k];
+            }
+            const double value = row[j] - sum;
             if (i == j) {
-                // Diagonal element
-                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
-                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
+                if (value <= 0.0) {
+                    return j;
                 }
-                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
-                if (val <= 0.0) {
-                    // Matrix is not positive definite
-                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
-                    return false;
-                }
-                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
+                row[j] = std::sqrt(value);
             } else {
-                // Off-diagonal element
-                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
-                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
+                row[j] = value / other[j];
+                matrix[j * n + i] = row[j];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    return n;
+}
+
+void solvePanel(double* matrix, size_t n, size_t rowFirst, size_t rowLast,
+                size_t first, size_t last) {
+    for (size_t i = rowFirst; i < rowLast; ++i) {
+        double* row = matrix + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = first; j < last; ++j) {
+            const double* diagonalRow = matrix + j * n;
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = first; k < j; ++k) {
+                sum += row[k] * diagonalRow[k];
+            }
+            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / diagonalRow[j];
+            matrix[j * n + i] = row[j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// A 4 x 8 register block reuses each panel load across several output entries.
+// Only tiles on/below the diagonal are updated; unused entries above the
+// diagonal inside a diagonal tile are harmless and simplify this hot kernel.
+void updateTile(double* matrix, size_t n, size_t rowFirst, size_t rowLast,
+                size_t colFirst, size_t colLast, size_t first, size_t last) {
+    size_t i = rowFirst;
+    for (; i + 4 <= rowLast; i += 4) {
+        double* row0 = matrix + i * n;
+        double* row1 = row0 + n;
+        double* row2 = row1 + n;
+        double* row3 = row2 + n;
+        size_t j = colFirst;
+        for (; j + 8 <= colLast; j += 8) {
+            double sum0[8] = {}, sum1[8] = {}, sum2[8] = {}, sum3[8] = {};
+            for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
+                const double* transposed = matrix + k * n + j;
+                const double a0 = row0[k], a1 = row1[k];
+                const double a2 = row2[k], a3 = row3[k];
+                #pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
+                    const double b = transposed[c];
+                    sum0[c] += a0 * b;
+                    sum1[c] += a1 * b;
+                    sum2[c] += a2 * b;
+                    sum3[c] += a3 * b;
                 }
-                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
             }
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
+                row0[j + c] -= sum0[c];
+                row1[j + c] -= sum1[c];
+                row2[j + c] -= sum2[c];
+                row3[j + c] -= sum3[c];
+            }
+        }
+        for (; j < colLast; ++j) {
+            const double* other = matrix + j * n;
+            double sum0 = 0.0, sum1 = 0.0, sum2 = 0.0, sum3 = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum0,sum1,sum2,sum3)
+            for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
+                sum0 += row0[k] * other[k];
+                sum1 += row1[k] * other[k];
+                sum2 += row2[k] * other[k];
+                sum3 += row3[k] * other[k];
+            }
+            row0[j] -= sum0;
+            row1[j] -= sum1;
+            row2[j] -= sum2;
+            row3[j] -= sum3;
+        }
+    }
+    for (; i < rowLast; ++i) {
+        double* row = matrix + i * n;
+        for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
+            const double a = row[k];
+            const double* transposed = matrix + k * n;
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t j = colFirst; j < colLast; ++j) {
+                row[j] -= a * transposed[j];
+            }
         }
-        
+    }
+}
+
+} // namespace
+
+// Tiled, right-looking Cholesky. Dependencies are keyed by each lower tile's
+// first element; all tasks are siblings generated by the same single region.
+// There is no global barrier between panels, so the next diagonal can start
+// while independent trailing updates are still running.
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    double* matrix = A.data();
+    std::atomic<bool> failed{false};
+    size_t failedDiagonal = n;
+
+    #pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        #pragma omp single
+        {
+            for (size_t first = 0; first < n; first += tileSize) {
+                const size_t last = std::min(first + tileSize, n);
+                #pragma omp task firstprivate(first,last) \
+                    depend(inout:matrix[first*n+first]) shared(failed,failedDiagonal)
+                {
+                    if (!failed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                        const size_t bad = factorDiagonal(matrix, n, first, last);
+                        if (bad != n) {
+                            failedDiagonal = bad;
+                            failed.store(true, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+                for (size_t row = last; row < n; row += tileSize) {
+                    const size_t rowLast = std::min(row + tileSize, n);
+                    #pragma omp task firstprivate(row,rowLast,first,last) \
+                        depend(in:matrix[first*n+first]) \
+                        depend(inout:matrix[row*n+first]) shared(failed)
+                    {
+                        if (!failed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                            solvePanel(matrix, n, row, rowLast, first, last);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+                // Issue nearer diagonal tiles first to shorten the critical path.
+                for (size_t col = last; col < n; col += tileSize) {
+                    const size_t colLast = std::min(col + tileSize, n);
+                    for (size_t row = col; row < n; row += tileSize) {
+                        const size_t rowLast = std::min(row + tileSize, n);
+                        #pragma omp task firstprivate(row,rowLast,col,colLast,first,last) \
+                            depend(in:matrix[row*n+first],matrix[col*n+first]) \
+                            depend(inout:matrix[row*n+col]) shared(failed)
+                        {
+                            if (!failed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                                updateTile(matrix, n, row, rowLast, col, colLast, first, last);
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        // The single region's implicit barrier also waits for its tasks.
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            std::fill(matrix + i * n + i + 1, matrix + (i + 1) * n, 0.0);
+        }
+    }
+    if (failed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", failedDiagonal);
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    return true;
+}
+
+// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix.
+void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
+    unsigned int seed = 42;
+
+    // Keep the original random stream: changing seeds per thread changes A.
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
+        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
+    }
+
+    // Compute each symmetric pair once. Dynamic rows balance the triangular
+    // workload; SIMD reductions accelerate the contiguous inner products.
+    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic,1)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+        const double* row = B.data() + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
+            const double* other = B.data() + j * n;
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
+                sum += row[k] * other[k];
+            }
+            if (i == j) {
+                sum += n;
+            }
+            A[i * n + j] = sum;
+            A[j * n + i] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
+    double maxError = 0.0;
+    double relError = 0.0;
+
+    // L is lower triangular. Entries beyond min(i,j) contribute only zero;
+    // compare both symmetric entries directly without a reconstruction buffer.
+    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic,1) reduction(max:maxError,relError)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+        const double* row = L.data() + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
+            const double* other = L.data() + j * n;
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k <= j; ++k) {
+                sum += row[k] * other[k];
+            }
+            const double original = A_orig[i * n + j];
+            const double transposed = A_orig[j * n + i];
+            const double error = std::fabs(sum - original);
+            const double transposeError = std::fabs(sum - transposed);
+            maxError = std::max(maxError, std::max(error, transposeError));
+            relError = std::max(relError, error / (std::fabs(original) + 1e-10));
+            relError = std::max(relError, transposeError / (std::fabs(transposed) + 1e-10));
+        }
+    }
+
+    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", maxError);
+    printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", relError);
+
+    if (relError > 1e-6) {
+        printf("Validation failed: relative error too large\n");
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    return true;
+}

#include <algorithm>
#include <atomic>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include <omp.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

namespace {

constexpr size_t tileSize = 64;

// The upper triangle temporarily holds the transpose of completed panels.
// This lets the update kernel vectorize over contiguous columns without packing
// another copy of the matrix. Every tile task also owns its transposed tile.
size_t factorDiagonal(double* matrix, size_t n, size_t first, size_t last) {
    for (size_t i = first; i < last; ++i) {
        double* row = matrix + i * n;
        for (size_t j = first; j <= i; ++j) {
            const double* other = matrix + j * n;
            double sum = 0.0;
            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
            for (size_t k = first; k < j; ++k) {
                sum += row[k] * other[k];
            }
            const double value = row[j] - sum;
            if (i == j) {
                if (value <= 0.0) {
                    return j;
                }
                row[j] = std::sqrt(value);
            } else {
                row[j] = value / other[j];
                matrix[j * n + i] = row[j];
            }
        }
    }
    return n;
}

void solvePanel(double* matrix, size_t n, size_t rowFirst, size_t rowLast,
                size_t first, size_t last) {
    for (size_t i = rowFirst; i < rowLast; ++i) {
        double* row = matrix + i * n;
        for (size_t j = first; j < last; ++j) {
            const double* diagonalRow = matrix + j * n;
            double sum = 0.0;
            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
            for (size_t k = first; k < j; ++k) {
                sum += row[k] * diagonalRow[k];
            }
            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / diagonalRow[j];
            matrix[j * n + i] = row[j];
        }
    }
}

// A 4 x 8 register block reuses each panel load across several output entries.
// Only tiles on/below the diagonal are updated; unused entries above the
// diagonal inside a diagonal tile are harmless and simplify this hot kernel.
void updateTile(double* matrix, size_t n, size_t rowFirst, size_t rowLast,
                size_t colFirst, size_t colLast, size_t first, size_t last) {
    size_t i = rowFirst;
    for (; i + 4 <= rowLast; i += 4) {
        double* row0 = matrix + i * n;
        double* row1 = row0 + n;
        double* row2 = row1 + n;
        double* row3 = row2 + n;
        size_t j = colFirst;
        for (; j + 8 <= colLast; j += 8) {
            double sum0[8] = {}, sum1[8] = {}, sum2[8] = {}, sum3[8] = {};
            for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
                const double* transposed = matrix + k * n + j;
                const double a0 = row0[k], a1 = row1[k];
                const double a2 = row2[k], a3 = row3[k];
                #pragma omp simd
                for (size_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
                    const double b = transposed[c];
                    sum0[c] += a0 * b;
                    sum1[c] += a1 * b;
                    sum2[c] += a2 * b;
                    sum3[c] += a3 * b;
                }
            }
            #pragma omp simd
            for (size_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
                row0[j + c] -= sum0[c];
                row1[j + c] -= sum1[c];
                row2[j + c] -= sum2[c];
                row3[j + c] -= sum3[c];
            }
        }
        for (; j < colLast; ++j) {
            const double* other = matrix + j * n;
            double sum0 = 0.0, sum1 = 0.0, sum2 = 0.0, sum3 = 0.0;
            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum0,sum1,sum2,sum3)
            for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
                sum0 += row0[k] * other[k];
                sum1 += row1[k] * other[k];
                sum2 += row2[k] * other[k];
                sum3 += row3[k] * other[k];
            }
            row0[j] -= sum0;
            row1[j] -= sum1;
            row2[j] -= sum2;
            row3[j] -= sum3;
        }
    }
    for (; i < rowLast; ++i) {
        double* row = matrix + i * n;
        for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
            const double a = row[k];
            const double* transposed = matrix + k * n;
            #pragma omp simd
            for (size_t j = colFirst; j < colLast; ++j) {
                row[j] -= a * transposed[j];
            }
        }
    }
}

} // namespace

// Tiled, right-looking Cholesky. Dependencies are keyed by each lower tile's
// first element; all tasks are siblings generated by the same single region.
// There is no global barrier between panels, so the next diagonal can start
// while independent trailing updates are still running.

bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    double* matrix = A.data();
    std::atomic<bool> failed{false};
    size_t failedDiagonal = n;

    #pragma omp parallel
    {
        #pragma omp single
        {
            for (size_t first = 0; first < n; first += tileSize) {
                const size_t last = std::min(first + tileSize, n);
                #pragma omp task firstprivate(first,last) \
                    depend(inout:matrix[first*n+first]) shared(failed,failedDiagonal)
                {
                    if (!failed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
                        const size_t bad = factorDiagonal(matrix, n, first, last);
                        if (bad != n) {
                            failedDiagonal = bad;
                            failed.store(true, std::memory_order_relaxed);
                        }
                    }
                }
                for (size_t row = last; row < n; row += tileSize) {
                    const size_t rowLast = std::min(row + tileSize, n);
                    #pragma omp task firstprivate(row,rowLast,first,last) \
                        depend(in:matrix[first*n+first]) \
                        depend(inout:matrix[row*n+first]) shared(failed)
                    {
                        if (!failed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
                            solvePanel(matrix, n, row, rowLast, first, last);
                        }
                    }
                }
                // Issue nearer diagonal tiles first to shorten the critical path.
                for (size_t col = last; col < n; col += tileSize) {
                    const size_t colLast = std::min(col + tileSize, n);
                    for (size_t row = col; row < n; row += tileSize) {
                        const size_t rowLast = std::min(row + tileSize, n);
                        #pragma omp task firstprivate(row,rowLast,col,colLast,first,last) \
                            depend(in:matrix[row*n+first],matrix[col*n+first]) \
                            depend(inout:matrix[row*n+col]) shared(failed)
                        {
                            if (!failed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
                                updateTile(matrix, n, row, rowLast, col, colLast, first, last);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // The single region's implicit barrier also waits for its tasks.
        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            std::fill(matrix + i * n + i + 1, matrix + (i + 1) * n, 0.0);
        }
    }
    if (failed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", failedDiagonal);
        return false;
    }

    return true;
}

// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix.
void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
    unsigned int seed = 42;

    // Keep the original random stream: changing seeds per thread changes A.
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
    }

    // Compute each symmetric pair once. Dynamic rows balance the triangular
    // workload; SIMD reductions accelerate the contiguous inner products.
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic,1)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        const double* row = B.data() + i * n;
        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
            const double* other = B.data() + j * n;
            double sum = 0.0;
            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += row[k] * other[k];
            }
            if (i == j) {
                sum += n;
            }
            A[i * n + j] = sum;
            A[j * n + i] = sum;
        }
    }
}

bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
    double maxError = 0.0;
    double relError = 0.0;

    // L is lower triangular. Entries beyond min(i,j) contribute only zero;
    // compare both symmetric entries directly without a reconstruction buffer.
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic,1) reduction(max:maxError,relError)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        const double* row = L.data() + i * n;
        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
            const double* other = L.data() + j * n;
            double sum = 0.0;
            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
            for (size_t k = 0; k <= j; ++k) {
                sum += row[k] * other[k];
            }
            const double original = A_orig[i * n + j];
            const double transposed = A_orig[j * n + i];
            const double error = std::fabs(sum - original);
            const double transposeError = std::fabs(sum - transposed);
            maxError = std::max(maxError, std::max(error, transposeError));
            relError = std::max(relError, error / (std::fabs(original) + 1e-10));
            relError = std::max(relError, transposeError / (std::fabs(transposed) + 1e-10));
        }
    }

    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", maxError);
    printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", relError);

    if (relError > 1e-6) {
        printf("Validation failed: relative error too large\n");
        return false;
    }

    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t n = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            n = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Cholesky Decomposition Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", n, n);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrix
    std::vector<double> A(n * n);
    std::vector<double> A_orig;
    
    // Generate positive definite matrix
    printf("Generating positive definite matrix...\n");
    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(A, n);
    
    if (validate) {
        A_orig = A; // Save original for validation
    }
    
    // Perform Cholesky decomposition
    printf("Computing Cholesky decomposition...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    bool success = choleskyDecomposition(A, n);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    if (!success) {
        printf("Cholesky decomposition failed\n");
        return 1;
    }
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS (approximately n³/3 operations for Cholesky)
    double ops = (double)n * n * n / 3.0;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(A, "CholeskyL");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateCholesky(A, A_orig, n);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

+// Tiled, right-looking Cholesky. Dependencies are keyed by each lower tile's

+// while independent trailing updates are still running.

+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
